## Overview

| ### services ||
|---|---|
| [`AccountBudgetProposalService`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccountBudgetProposalService) | A service for managing account-level budgets through proposals. A proposal is a request to create a new budget or make changes to an existing one. Mutates: The CREATE operation creates a new proposal. UPDATE operations aren't supported. The REMOVE operation cancels a pending proposal. |

A billing setup is associated with a payments account. Billing-related activity for all billing setups associated with a particular payments account will appear on a single invoice generated monthly. Mutates: The REMOVE operation cancels a pending billing setup. The CREATE operation creates a new billing setup. |

| [`KeywordPlanAdGroupKeywordService`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordPlanAdGroupKeywordService) | Service to manage Keyword Plan ad group keywords. KeywordPlanAdGroup is required to add ad group keywords. Positive and negative keywords are supported. A maximum of 10,000 positive keywords are allowed per keyword plan. A maximum of 1,000 negative keywords are allower per keyword plan. This includes campaign negative keywords and ad group negative keywords. |
| [`KeywordPlanAdGroupService`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordPlanAdGroupService) | Service to manage Keyword Plan ad groups. |
| [`KeywordPlanCampaignKeywordService`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordPlanCampaignKeywordService) | Service to manage Keyword Plan campaign keywords. KeywordPlanCampaign is required to add the campaign keywords. Only negative keywords are supported. A maximum of 1000 negative keywords are allowed per plan. This includes both campaign negative keywords and ad group negative keywords. |
| [`KeywordPlanCampaignService`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordPlanCampaignService) | Service to manage Keyword Plan campaigns. |

| [`ShareablePreviewService`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ShareablePreviewService) | Service to generate Shareable Previews. Only Performance Max asset groups and certain YouTube video/audio ad formats are supported. Other ad types, such as Responsive Search Ads or Responsive Display Ads, are not supported and return an `UNSUPPORTED_AD_TYPE` error. The generated preview URLs cannot be embedded in an iframe because the response headers include `X-Frame-Options: deny`. |

| [`UserDataService`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/UserDataService) | Service to manage user data uploads. Any uploads made to a Customer Match list through this service will be eligible for matching as per the customer matching process. See <https://support.google.com/google-ads/answer/7474263>. However, the uploads made through this service will not be visible under the 'Segment members' section for the Customer Match List in the Google Ads UI. |

| [`BookCampaignsOperation`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BookCampaignsOperation) | Request message for the BookCampaigns action. Request including this operation can have a latency of up to 30 seconds. This feature is not publicly available. |
| [`BookCampaignsOperation.Campaign`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BookCampaignsOperation.Campaign) | A single campaign to book. |
| [`BookCampaignsResult`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BookCampaignsResult) | Response message for the BookCampaigns action. Note that if the response contains errors, the action response will not be returned, but a quote may still be returned in the ErrorDetails.reservation_error_details field. |
| [`GenerateShareablePreviewsOperation`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/GenerateShareablePreviewsOperation) | Operation to generate shareable previews. |
| [`GenerateShareablePreviewsResult`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/GenerateShareablePreviewsResult) | Result of the GenerateShareablePreviews action. |
| [`QuoteCampaignsOperation`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/QuoteCampaignsOperation) | Request message for the QuoteCampaigns action. Request including this operation can have a latency of up to 30 seconds. This feature is not publicly available. |
| [`QuoteCampaignsOperation.Campaign`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/QuoteCampaignsOperation.Campaign) | A campaign for which the quote is requested. |
| [`QuoteCampaignsResult`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/QuoteCampaignsResult) | The response of the QuoteCampaigns action, when the action is successful. Note that if the response contains errors, the action response will not be returned, but a quote may still be returned in the ErrorDetails.reservation_error_details field. |
| [`ShareablePreview`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ShareablePreview) | A shareable preview with its identifier. Only Performance Max asset groups and certain YouTube video/audio ad formats are supported. Other ad types, such as Responsive Search Ads or Responsive Display Ads, are not supported and return an `UNSUPPORTED_AD_TYPE` error. The generated preview URLs cannot be embedded in an iframe because the response headers include `X-Frame-Options: deny`. |
| [`ShareablePreviewResult`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ShareablePreviewResult) | Message to hold a shareable preview result. |

This field is only used by `https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ContentCreatorInsightsService`, `https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AudienceInsightsService`, and `https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ReachPlanService` APIs. |

| [`AppDeepLinkAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AppDeepLinkAsset) | An app deep link asset |
| [`AppEngagementAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AppEngagementAdInfo) | App engagement ads allow you to write text encouraging a specific action in the app, like checking in, making a purchase, or booking a flight. They allow you to send users to a specific part of your app where they can find what they're looking for easier and faster. |
| [`AppPaymentModelInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AppPaymentModelInfo) | An app payment model criterion. |
| [`AppPreRegistrationAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AppPreRegistrationAdInfo) | App pre-registration ads link to your app or game listing on Google Play, and can run on Google Play, on YouTube (in-stream only), and within other apps and mobile websites on the Display Network. It will help capture people's interest in your app or game and generate an early install base for your app or game before a launch. |

| [`AssetLinkPrimaryStatusDetails`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetLinkPrimaryStatusDetails) | Provides the detail of a PrimaryStatus. Each asset link has a PrimaryStatus value (e.g. NOT_ELIGIBLE, meaning not serving), and list of corroborating PrimaryStatusReasons (e.g. \[ASSET_DISAPPROVED\]). Each reason may have some additional details annotated with it. For instance, when the reason is ASSET_DISAPPROVED, the details field will contain additional information about the offline evaluation errors which led to the asset being disapproved. |

This bidding strategy is deprecated and cannot be created anymore. Use ManualCpc with enhanced_cpc_enabled set to true for equivalent functionality. |

| [`FlexibleRuleUserListInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/FlexibleRuleUserListInfo) | Flexible rule representation of visitors with one or multiple actions. The flexible user list is defined by two lists of operands -- inclusive_operands and exclusive_operands; each operand represents a set of users based on actions they took in a given timeframe. These lists of operands are combined with the AND_NOT operator, so that users represented by the inclusive operands are included in the user list, minus the users represented by the exclusive operands. |
| [`FrequencyCapEntry`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/FrequencyCapEntry) | A rule specifying the maximum number of times an ad (or some set of ads) can be shown to a user over a particular time period. |

| [`IpBlockInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/IpBlockInfo) | An IpBlock criterion used for excluding IP addresses. We support excluding individual IP addresses or CIDR blocks. Create one IpBlockInfo criterion for each individual IP address or CIDR block you want to exclude. You can exclude up to 500 IP addresses per campaign. For more details, see [Exclude IP addresses](https://support.google.com/google-ads/answer/2456098). IPv4 examples: - Individual address: 192.168.0.1 - Individual address as CIDR block: 192.168.0.1/32 - CIDR block: 192.168.0.0/24 IPv6 examples: - Individual address: 2001:db8:a0b:12f0::1 - Individual address as CIDR block: 2001:db8:a0b:12f0::1/128 - CIDR block: 2001:db8::/48 |
| [`ItemAttribute`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ItemAttribute) | Item attributes of the transaction. |

| [`KeywordConcept`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordConcept) | The concept for the keyword. |
| [`KeywordInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordInfo) | A keyword criterion. |

| [`KeywordPlanAggregateMetrics`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordPlanAggregateMetrics) | The aggregate metrics specification of the request. |
| [`KeywordPlanDeviceSearches`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordPlanDeviceSearches) | The total searches for the device type during the specified time period. |
| [`KeywordPlanHistoricalMetrics`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordPlanHistoricalMetrics) | Historical metrics specific to the targeting options selected. Targeting options include geographies, network, and so on. Refer to <https://support.google.com/google-ads/answer/3022575> for more details. |
| [`KeywordThemeInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KeywordThemeInfo) | A Smart Campaign keyword theme. |
| [`KnowledgeGraphAttributeMetadata`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/KnowledgeGraphAttributeMetadata) | Metadata associated with a Knowledge Graph Entity attribute. |
| [`LanguageInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LanguageInfo) | A language criterion. |
| [`LeadFormAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LeadFormAsset) | A Lead Form asset. |
| [`LeadFormCustomQuestionField`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LeadFormCustomQuestionField) | One custom question input field instance within a form. |
| [`LeadFormDeliveryMethod`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LeadFormDeliveryMethod) | A configuration of how leads are delivered to the advertiser. |
| [`LeadFormField`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LeadFormField) | One input field instance within a form. |
| [`LeadFormSingleChoiceAnswers`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LeadFormSingleChoiceAnswers) | Defines possible answers for a single choice question, usually presented as a single-choice drop-down list. |
| [`LegacyAppInstallAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LegacyAppInstallAdInfo) | A legacy app install ad that only can be used by a few select customers. |
| [`LegacyResponsiveDisplayAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LegacyResponsiveDisplayAdInfo) | A legacy responsive display ad. Ads of this type are labeled 'Responsive ads' in the Google Ads UI. |
| [`LifeEventInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LifeEventInfo) | Represents a life event criterion. |
| [`LifeEventSegment`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LifeEventSegment) | Live event segment. |
| [`LineupAttributeMetadata`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LineupAttributeMetadata) | Metadata associated with a Lineup attribute. |
| [`LineupAttributeMetadata.SampleChannel`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LineupAttributeMetadata.SampleChannel) | A YouTube channel returned as an example of the content in a lineup. |
| [`ListingDimensionInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ListingDimensionInfo) | Listing dimensions for listing group criterion. |
| [`ListingDimensionPath`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ListingDimensionPath) | The path of dimensions defining a listing group. |
| [`ListingGroupInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ListingGroupInfo) | A listing group criterion. |
| [`ListingScopeInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ListingScopeInfo) | A listing scope criterion. |
| [`LocalAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LocalAdInfo) | A local ad. |
| [`LocalServiceIdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LocalServiceIdInfo) | A Local Services Ads service ID. Represents a service type (such as install_faucet) that a Local Services Campaign can target. |
| [`LocalServicesDocumentReadOnly`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LocalServicesDocumentReadOnly) | A Local Services Document with read only accessible data. |
| [`LocalServicesPhoneNumber`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LocalServicesPhoneNumber) | Phone number associated with the provider. |
| [`LocationAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LocationAsset) | A location asset. |
| [`LocationAttributeMetadata`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LocationAttributeMetadata) | Metadata associated with a Location attribute. |
| [`LocationGroupInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LocationGroupInfo) | A radius around a list of locations specified through a feed or assetSet. |
| [`LocationInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LocationInfo) | A location criterion. |
| [`LocationSet`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LocationSet) | Data related to location set. One of the Google Business Profile (previously known as Google My Business) data, Chain data, and map location data need to be specified. |
| [`LogicalUserListInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LogicalUserListInfo) | Represents a user list that is a custom combination of user lists. |
| [`LogicalUserListOperandInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LogicalUserListOperandInfo) | Operand of logical user list that consists of a user list. |
| [`LookalikeUserListInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/LookalikeUserListInfo) | LookalikeUserlist, composed of users similar to those of a configurable seed (set of UserLists) |
| [`ManualCpa`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ManualCpa) | Manual bidding strategy that allows advertiser to set the bid per advertiser-specified action. |
| [`ManualCpc`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ManualCpc) | Manual click-based bidding where user pays per click. |
| [`ManualCpm`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ManualCpm) | Manual impression-based bidding where user pays per thousand impressions. |
| [`ManualCpv`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ManualCpv) | View based bidding where user pays per video view. |
| [`MapsLocationInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MapsLocationInfo) | Wrapper for place ids |
| [`MapsLocationSet`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MapsLocationSet) | Wrapper for multiple maps location sync data |
| [`MaximizeConversionValue`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MaximizeConversionValue) | An automated bidding strategy to help get the most conversion value for your campaigns while spending your budget. |
| [`MaximizeConversions`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MaximizeConversions) | An automated bidding strategy to help get the most conversions for your campaigns while spending your budget. |
| [`MediaBundleAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MediaBundleAsset) | A MediaBundle asset. |
| [`MetricGoal`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MetricGoal) | A metric goal for an experiment. |
| [`Metrics`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Metrics) | Metrics data. |
| [`MobileAppAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MobileAppAsset) | An asset representing a mobile app. |
| [`MobileAppCategoryInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MobileAppCategoryInfo) | A mobile app category criterion. |
| [`MobileApplicationInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MobileApplicationInfo) | A mobile application criterion. |
| [`MobileDeviceInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MobileDeviceInfo) | A mobile device criterion. |
| [`Money`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Money) | Represents a price in a particular currency. |
| [`MonthlySearchVolume`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/MonthlySearchVolume) | Monthly search volume. |
| [`NegativeKeywordListInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/NegativeKeywordListInfo) | A Negative Keyword List criterion. Represents a shared set of negative keywords that can be excluded at the account-level. Only one negative keyword list criterion can be attached per account. |
| [`OfflineUserAddressInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/OfflineUserAddressInfo) | Address identifier of offline data. |
| [`OperatingSystemVersionInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/OperatingSystemVersionInfo) | Represents an operating system version to be targeted. |
| [`OptimizeAssetsExperimentInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/OptimizeAssetsExperimentInfo) | Configuration for an Optimize Assets experiment. |
| [`PageFeedAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PageFeedAsset) | A Page Feed asset. |
| [`ParentalStatusDimension`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ParentalStatusDimension) | Dimension specifying users by their parental status. |
| [`ParentalStatusInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ParentalStatusInfo) | A parental status criterion. |
| [`PercentCpc`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PercentCpc) | A bidding strategy where bids are a fraction of the advertised price for some good or service. |
| [`PercentCpcBidSimulationPoint`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PercentCpcBidSimulationPoint) | Projected metrics for a specific percent CPC amount. Only Hotel advertising channel type supports this field. |
| [`PercentCpcBidSimulationPointList`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PercentCpcBidSimulationPointList) | A container for simulation points for simulations of type PERCENT_CPC_BID. |
| [`PlacementInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PlacementInfo) | A placement criterion. This can be used to modify bids for sites when targeting the content network. |
| [`PlacementListInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PlacementListInfo) | A Placement List criterion. Represents a shared set of placements that can be excluded at the account-level. |
| [`PolicySummary`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicySummary) | Contains policy summary information. |
| [`PolicyTopicConstraint`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicConstraint) | Describes the effect on serving that a policy topic entry will have. |
| [`PolicyTopicConstraint.CountryConstraint`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicConstraint.CountryConstraint) | Indicates that a resource's ability to serve in a particular country is constrained. |
| [`PolicyTopicConstraint.CountryConstraintList`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicConstraint.CountryConstraintList) | A list of countries where a resource's serving is constrained. |
| [`PolicyTopicConstraint.ResellerConstraint`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicConstraint.ResellerConstraint) | Indicates that a policy topic was constrained due to disapproval of the website for reseller purposes. |
| [`PolicyTopicEntry`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicEntry) | Policy finding attached to a resource (for example, alcohol policy associated with a site that sells alcohol). Each PolicyTopicEntry has a topic that indicates the specific ads policy the entry is about and a type to indicate the effect that the entry will have on serving. It may optionally have one or more evidences that indicate the reason for the finding. It may also optionally have one or more constraints that provide details about how serving may be restricted. |
| [`PolicyTopicEvidence`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicEvidence) | Additional information that explains a policy finding. |
| [`PolicyTopicEvidence.DestinationMismatch`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicEvidence.DestinationMismatch) | Evidence of mismatches between the URLs of a resource. |
| [`PolicyTopicEvidence.DestinationNotWorking`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicEvidence.DestinationNotWorking) | Evidence details when the destination is returning an HTTP error code or isn't functional in all locations for commonly used devices. |
| [`PolicyTopicEvidence.DestinationTextList`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicEvidence.DestinationTextList) | A list of strings found in a destination page that caused a policy finding. |
| [`PolicyTopicEvidence.TextList`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicEvidence.TextList) | A list of fragments of text that violated a policy. |
| [`PolicyTopicEvidence.WebsiteList`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyTopicEvidence.WebsiteList) | A list of websites that caused a policy finding. Used for ONE_WEBSITE_PER_AD_GROUP policy topic, for example. In case there are more than five websites, only the top five (those that appear in resources the most) will be listed here. |
| [`PolicyValidationParameter`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyValidationParameter) | Parameter for controlling how policy exemption is done. |
| [`PolicyViolationKey`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PolicyViolationKey) | Key of the violation. The key is used for referring to a violation when filing an exemption request. |
| [`PriceAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PriceAsset) | An asset representing a list of price offers. |
| [`PriceOffering`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PriceOffering) | A single price offering within a PriceAsset. |
| [`ProductBrandInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductBrandInfo) | Brand of the product. |
| [`ProductCategoryInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductCategoryInfo) | Category of a product offer. |
| [`ProductChannelExclusivityInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductChannelExclusivityInfo) | Availability of a product offer. |
| [`ProductChannelInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductChannelInfo) | Locality of a product offer. |
| [`ProductConditionInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductConditionInfo) | Condition of a product offer. |
| [`ProductCustomAttributeInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductCustomAttributeInfo) | Custom attribute of a product offer. |
| [`ProductGroupingInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductGroupingInfo) | Grouping of a product offer. This listing dimension is deprecated and it is supported only in Display campaigns. |
| [`ProductItemIdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductItemIdInfo) | Item id of a product offer. |
| [`ProductLabelsInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductLabelsInfo) | Labels of a product offer. This listing dimension is deprecated and it is supported only in Display campaigns. |
| [`ProductLegacyConditionInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductLegacyConditionInfo) | Legacy condition of a product offer. This listing dimension is deprecated and it is supported only in Display campaigns. |
| [`ProductTypeFullInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductTypeFullInfo) | Full type of a product offer. This listing dimension is deprecated and it is supported only in Display campaigns. |
| [`ProductTypeInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProductTypeInfo) | Type of a product offer. |
| [`PromotionAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PromotionAsset) | A Promotion asset. |
| [`PromotionBarcodeInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PromotionBarcodeInfo) | Barcode info to generate promotion barcode. |
| [`PromotionQrCodeInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/PromotionQrCodeInfo) | QR code info to generate promotion QR code. |
| [`ProximityInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ProximityInfo) | A Proximity criterion. The geo point and radius determine what geographical area is included. The address is a description of the geo point that does not affect ad serving. There are two ways to create a proximity. First, by setting an address and radius. The geo point will be automatically computed. Second, by setting a geo point and radius. The address is an optional label that won't be validated. |
| [`RealTimeBiddingSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/RealTimeBiddingSetting) | Settings for Real-Time Bidding, a feature only available for campaigns targeting the Ad Exchange network. |
| [`ResponsiveDisplayAdControlSpec`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ResponsiveDisplayAdControlSpec) | Specification for various creative controls for a responsive display ad. |
| [`ResponsiveDisplayAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ResponsiveDisplayAdInfo) | A responsive display ad. |
| [`ResponsiveSearchAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ResponsiveSearchAdInfo) | A responsive search ad. Responsive search ads let you create an ad that adapts to show more text, and more relevant messages, to your customers. Enter multiple headlines and descriptions when creating a responsive search ad, and over time, Google Ads will automatically test different combinations and learn which combinations perform best. By adapting your ad's content to more closely match potential customers' search terms, responsive search ads may improve your campaign's performance. More information at <https://support.google.com/google-ads/answer/7684791> |
| [`RetailFilter`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/RetailFilter) | Represents a Retail Filter. |
| [`RetailFilterBundle`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/RetailFilterBundle) | Represents a Retail Filter Bundle, used to link an ad group to a shared set containing retail filters. |
| [`RetailFilterExpression`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/RetailFilterExpression) | Represents a Retail Filter Expression. Tags grouped within an expression are joined with OR operator. A product needs to have any of these tags to satisfy expression condition. |
| [`RetailTag`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/RetailTag) | Represents a Retail Filter Tag. |
| [`RuleBasedUserListInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/RuleBasedUserListInfo) | Representation of a userlist that is generated by a rule. |
| [`SearchThemeInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/SearchThemeInfo) | A Search Theme criterion only on Performance Max campaign. Represents a keyword-like advertiser input. |
| [`SearchVolumeRange`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/SearchVolumeRange) | Search volume range. Actual search volume falls within this range. |
| [`Segments`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Segments) | Segment only fields. |
| [`ShoppingComparisonListingAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ShoppingComparisonListingAdInfo) | A Shopping Comparison Listing ad. |
| [`ShoppingLoyalty`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ShoppingLoyalty) | The shopping loyalty related data. Shopping utilizes this data to provide users with a better experience. Accessible only to merchants on the allow-list. |
| [`ShoppingProductAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ShoppingProductAdInfo) | A standard Shopping ad. |
| [`ShoppingSmartAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ShoppingSmartAdInfo) | A Smart Shopping ad. |
| [`SimilarUserListInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/SimilarUserListInfo) | SimilarUserList is a list of users which are similar to users from another UserList. These lists are read-only and automatically created by Google. |
| [`SitelinkAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/SitelinkAsset) | A Sitelink asset. |
| [`SitelinkFeedItem`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/SitelinkFeedItem) | Represents a sitelink. |
| [`SkAdNetworkSourceApp`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/SkAdNetworkSourceApp) | A SkAdNetworkSourceApp segment. |
| [`SmartCampaignAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/SmartCampaignAdInfo) | A Smart campaign ad. |
| [`StoreAttribute`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/StoreAttribute) | Store attributes of the transaction. |
| [`StoreSalesMetadata`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/StoreSalesMetadata) | Metadata for Store Sales Direct. |
| [`StoreSalesThirdPartyMetadata`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/StoreSalesThirdPartyMetadata) | Metadata for a third party Store Sales. This product is only for customers on the allow-list. Contact your Google business development representative for details on the upload configuration. |
| [`StructuredSnippetAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/StructuredSnippetAsset) | A Structured Snippet asset. |
| [`SyntheticContentAttestation`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/SyntheticContentAttestation) | Represents an attestation about synthetic content from a single source. |
| [`SyntheticContentInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/SyntheticContentInfo) | Container for synthetic content attestations from different sources, such as the advertiser and Google systems. |
| [`TagSnippet`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TagSnippet) | The site tag and event snippet pair for a TrackingCodeType. |
| [`TargetCpa`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetCpa) | An automated bid strategy that sets bids to help get as many conversions as possible at the target cost-per-acquisition (CPA) you set. |
| [`TargetCpaSimulationPoint`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetCpaSimulationPoint) | Projected metrics for a specific target CPA amount. |
| [`TargetCpaSimulationPointList`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetCpaSimulationPointList) | A container for simulation points for simulations of type TARGET_CPA. |
| [`TargetCpc`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetCpc) | An automated bidding strategy that sets bids to help get as many clicks as possible at the target cost-per-click (CPC) you set. |
| [`TargetCpm`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetCpm) | Target CPM (cost per thousand impressions) is an automated bidding strategy that sets bids to optimize performance given the target CPM you set. |
| [`TargetCpmTargetFrequencyGoal`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetCpmTargetFrequencyGoal) | Target Frequency bidding goal details. |
| [`TargetCpv`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetCpv) | Target CPV (cost per view) bidding strategy. An automated bidding strategy that sets bids to optimize performance given the target CPV you set. |
| [`TargetImpressionShare`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetImpressionShare) | An automated bidding strategy that sets bids so that a certain percentage of search ads are shown at the top of the first page (or other targeted location). |
| [`TargetImpressionShareSimulationPoint`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetImpressionShareSimulationPoint) | Projected metrics for a specific target impression share value. |
| [`TargetImpressionShareSimulationPointList`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetImpressionShareSimulationPointList) | A container for simulation points for simulations of type TARGET_IMPRESSION_SHARE. |
| [`TargetRestriction`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetRestriction) | The list of per-targeting-dimension targeting settings. |
| [`TargetRestrictionOperation`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetRestrictionOperation) | Operation to be performed on a target restriction list in a mutate. |
| [`TargetRestrictionOperation.Operator`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetRestrictionOperation.Operator) | The operator. |
| [`TargetRoas`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetRoas) | An automated bidding strategy that helps you maximize revenue while averaging a specific target return on ad spend (ROAS). |
| [`TargetRoasSimulationPoint`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetRoasSimulationPoint) | Projected metrics for a specific target ROAS amount. |
| [`TargetRoasSimulationPointList`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetRoasSimulationPointList) | A container for simulation points for simulations of type TARGET_ROAS. |
| [`TargetSpend`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetSpend) | An automated bid strategy that sets your bids to help get as many clicks as possible within your budget. |
| [`TargetingSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TargetingSetting) | Settings for the targeting-related features, at the campaign and ad group levels. For more details about the targeting setting, visit <https://support.google.com/google-ads/answer/7365594> |
| [`TextAdInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TextAdInfo) | A text ad. |
| [`TextAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TextAsset) | A Text asset. |
| [`TextLabel`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TextLabel) | A type of label displaying text on a colored background. |
| [`ThirdPartyIntegrationPartnerData`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/ThirdPartyIntegrationPartnerData) | Contains third party measurement partner related data for video campaigns. |
| [`TopicInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/TopicInfo) | A topic criterion. Use topics to target or exclude placements in the Google Display Network based on the category into which the placement falls (for example, "Pets \& Animals/Pets/Dogs"). |

Provides reasons into why an asset group is not serving or not serving optimally. It will be empty when the asset group is serving without issues. |

| [`AssetLinkPrimaryStatus`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetLinkPrimaryStatusEnum.AssetLinkPrimaryStatus) | Enum Provides insight into why an asset is not serving or not serving at full capacity for a particular link level. There could be one status with multiple reasons. For example, a sitelink might be paused by the user, but also limited in serving due to violation of an alcohol policy. In this case, the PrimaryStatus will be returned as PAUSED, since the asset's effective status is determined by its paused state. |
| [`AssetLinkPrimaryStatusReason`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetLinkPrimaryStatusReasonEnum.AssetLinkPrimaryStatusReason) | Enum Provides insight into why an asset is not serving or not serving at full capacity for a particular link level. These reasons are aggregated to determine a final PrimaryStatus. For example, a sitelink might be paused by the user, but also limited in serving due to violation of an alcohol policy. In this case, the PrimaryStatus will be returned as PAUSED, since the asset's effective status is determined by its paused state. |

A single `MutateCustomerLabelsRequest` can only modify labels for the single customer account specified in the request. To apply a label to multiple different accounts, separate `MutateCustomerLabelsRequest` calls must be made. |

| [`AccessibleBiddingStrategy`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccessibleBiddingStrategy) | Represents a view of BiddingStrategies owned by and shared with the customer. In contrast to BiddingStrategy, this resource includes strategies owned by managers of the customer and shared with this customer - in addition to strategies owned by this customer. This resource does not provide metrics and only exposes a limited subset of the BiddingStrategy attributes. |
| [`AccessibleBiddingStrategy.MaximizeConversionValue`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccessibleBiddingStrategy.MaximizeConversionValue) | An automated bidding strategy to help get the most conversion value for your campaigns while spending your budget. |
| [`AccessibleBiddingStrategy.MaximizeConversions`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccessibleBiddingStrategy.MaximizeConversions) | An automated bidding strategy to help get the most conversions for your campaigns while spending your budget. |
| [`AccessibleBiddingStrategy.TargetCpa`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccessibleBiddingStrategy.TargetCpa) | An automated bid strategy that sets bids to help get as many conversions as possible at the target cost-per-acquisition (CPA) you set. |
| [`AccessibleBiddingStrategy.TargetImpressionShare`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccessibleBiddingStrategy.TargetImpressionShare) | An automated bidding strategy that sets bids so that a certain percentage of search ads are shown at the top of the first page (or other targeted location). |
| [`AccessibleBiddingStrategy.TargetRoas`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccessibleBiddingStrategy.TargetRoas) | An automated bidding strategy that helps you maximize revenue while averaging a specific target return on ad spend (ROAS). |
| [`AccessibleBiddingStrategy.TargetSpend`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccessibleBiddingStrategy.TargetSpend) | An automated bid strategy that sets your bids to help get as many clicks as possible within your budget. |
| [`AccountBudget`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccountBudget) | An account-level budget. It contains information about the budget itself, as well as the most recently approved changes to the budget and proposed changes that are pending approval. The proposed changes that are pending approval, if any, are found in 'pending_proposal'. Effective details about the budget are found in fields prefixed 'approved_', 'adjusted_' and those without a prefix. Since some effective details may differ from what the user had originally requested (for example, spending limit), these differences are juxtaposed through 'proposed_', 'approved_', and possibly 'adjusted_' fields. This resource is mutated using AccountBudgetProposal and cannot be mutated directly. A budget may have at most one pending proposal at any given time. It is read through pending_proposal. Once approved, a budget may be subject to adjustments, such as credit adjustments. Adjustments create differences between the 'approved' and 'adjusted' fields, which would otherwise be identical. |
| [`AccountBudget.PendingAccountBudgetProposal`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccountBudget.PendingAccountBudgetProposal) | A pending proposal associated with the enclosing account-level budget, if applicable. |
| [`AccountBudgetProposal`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccountBudgetProposal) | An account-level budget proposal. All fields prefixed with 'proposed' may not necessarily be applied directly. For example, proposed spending limits may be adjusted before their application. This is true if the 'proposed' field has an 'approved' counterpart, for example, spending limits. Note that the proposal type (proposal_type) changes which fields are required and which must remain empty. |
| [`AccountLink`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AccountLink) | Represents the data sharing connection between a Google Ads account and another account |
| [`Ad`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Ad) | An ad. |

| [`AdGroup.DemandGenAdGroupSettings.DemandGenChannelControls.DemandGenSelectedChannels`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroup.DemandGenAdGroupSettings.DemandGenChannelControls.DemandGenSelectedChannels) | Explicitly selected channels for channel controls in Demand Gen ad groups. |
| [`AdGroup.VerticalAdsFormatSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroup.VerticalAdsFormatSetting) | Vertical ads setting feature to enable/disable ad group format controls in search campaigns. |
| [`AdGroup.VideoAdGroupSettings`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroup.VideoAdGroupSettings) | Settings for video ad groups. |
| [`AdGroup.VideoAdGroupSettings.VideoAdSequenceStepSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroup.VideoAdGroupSettings.VideoAdSequenceStepSetting) | The video ads sequence step settings containing step ID. |

| [`AdGroupAdAssetCombinationView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroupAdAssetCombinationView) | A view on the usage of ad group ad asset combination. Now we only support AdGroupAdAssetCombinationView for Responsive Search Ads, with more ad types planned for the future. |
| [`AdGroupAdAssetPolicySummary`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroupAdAssetPolicySummary) | Contains policy information for an ad group ad asset. |
| [`AdGroupAdAssetView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroupAdAssetView) | Represents a link between an AdGroupAd and an Asset. This view provides insights into the performance of assets within specific ads. AdGroupAdAssetView supports the following ad types: - App Ads - Demand Gen campaigns - Responsive Search Ads It does not support Responsive Display Ads. |

| [`AdGroupAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroupAsset) | A link between an ad group and an asset. |

| [`AdGroupCriterionSimulation`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroupCriterionSimulation) | An ad group criterion simulation. Supported combinations of advertising channel type, criterion type, simulation type, and simulation modification method are detailed below respectively. Hotel AdGroupCriterion simulation operations starting in V5. 1. DISPLAY - KEYWORD - CPC_BID - UNIFORM 2. SEARCH - KEYWORD - CPC_BID - UNIFORM 3. SHOPPING - LISTING_GROUP - CPC_BID - UNIFORM 4. HOTEL - LISTING_GROUP - CPC_BID - UNIFORM 5. HOTEL - LISTING_GROUP - PERCENT_CPC_BID - UNIFORM |
| [`AdGroupCustomizer`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroupCustomizer) | A customizer value for the associated CustomizerAttribute at the AdGroup level. |
| [`AdGroupLabel`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroupLabel) | A relationship between an ad group and a label. |
| [`AdGroupSimulation`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdGroupSimulation) | An ad group simulation. Supported combinations of advertising channel type, simulation type and simulation modification method is detailed below respectively. 1. SEARCH - CPC_BID - DEFAULT 2. SEARCH - CPC_BID - UNIFORM 3. SEARCH - TARGET_CPA - UNIFORM 4. SEARCH - TARGET_ROAS - UNIFORM 5. DISPLAY - CPC_BID - DEFAULT 6. DISPLAY - CPC_BID - UNIFORM 7. DISPLAY - TARGET_CPA - UNIFORM |
| [`AdParameter`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdParameter) | An ad parameter that is used to update numeric values (such as prices or inventory levels) in any text line of an ad (including URLs). There can be a maximum of two AdParameters per ad group criterion. (One with parameter_index = 1 and one with parameter_index = 2.) In the ad the parameters are referenced by a placeholder of the form "{param#:value}". For example, "{param1:$17}" |
| [`AdScheduleView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdScheduleView) | An ad schedule view summarizes the performance of campaigns by AdSchedule criteria. |
| [`AdStrengthActionItem`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdStrengthActionItem) | An action item to improve the ad strength of an asset group. |
| [`AdStrengthActionItem.AddAssetDetails`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdStrengthActionItem.AddAssetDetails) | The details of the asset to add. |
| [`AdvertisingPartnerIdentifier`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdvertisingPartnerIdentifier) | The identifier for the Advertising Partner Google Ads account. |
| [`AdvertisingPartnerLinkInvitationIdentifier`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdvertisingPartnerLinkInvitationIdentifier) | The identifier for the Advertising Partner Google Ads account. |
| [`AdvertisingPartnerLinkInvitationProperties`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdvertisingPartnerLinkInvitationProperties) | Properties specific to an Advertising Partner link invitation. |
| [`AdvertisingPartnerProperties`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AdvertisingPartnerProperties) | Properties specific to an Advertising Partner link. |
| [`AgeRangeView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AgeRangeView) | An age range view. Represents the view of a customer's performance metrics (like impressions and clicks) aggregated by age range. All statistics are aggregated at the ad group level. Note: While you can segment metrics by age range using `age_range_view` or by gender using `gender_view`, the Google Ads API does not support segmenting metrics by both age range and gender simultaneously in a single query. Analyzing performance across both dimensions combined is not supported in the Google Ads API. |
| [`AiMaxSearchTermAdCombinationView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AiMaxSearchTermAdCombinationView) | AiMaxSearchTermAdCombinationView Resource. |
| [`AndroidPrivacySharedKeyGoogleAdGroup`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AndroidPrivacySharedKeyGoogleAdGroup) | An Android privacy shared key view for Google ad group key. |
| [`AndroidPrivacySharedKeyGoogleCampaign`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AndroidPrivacySharedKeyGoogleCampaign) | An Android privacy shared key view for Google campaign key. |
| [`AndroidPrivacySharedKeyGoogleNetworkType`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AndroidPrivacySharedKeyGoogleNetworkType) | An Android privacy shared key view for Google network type key. |
| [`AppTopCombinationView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AppTopCombinationView) | A view resource in the App Top Combination Report. |
| [`AppliedIncentive`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AppliedIncentive) | Represents an applied incentive. |
| [`Asset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Asset) | Asset is a part of an ad which can be shared across multiple ads. It can be an image (ImageAsset), a video (YoutubeVideoAsset), etc. Assets are immutable and cannot be removed. To stop an asset from serving, remove the asset from the entity that is using it. |
| [`AssetCoverage`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetCoverage) | Information about the asset coverage of an asset group. |
| [`AssetFieldTypePolicySummary`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetFieldTypePolicySummary) | Contains policy information for an asset under AssetFieldType context. |
| [`AssetFieldTypeView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetFieldTypeView) | An asset field type view. This view reports non-overcounted metrics for each asset field type when the asset is used as extension. |
| [`AssetGroup`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetGroup) | An asset group. AssetGroupAsset is used to link an asset to the asset group. AssetGroupSignal is used to associate a signal to an asset group. |
| [`AssetGroupAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetGroupAsset) | AssetGroupAsset is the link between an asset and an asset group. Adding an AssetGroupAsset links an asset with an asset group. |
| [`AssetGroupAssetCombinationData`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetGroupAssetCombinationData) | Asset group asset combination data |
| [`AssetGroupListingGroupFilter`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetGroupListingGroupFilter) | AssetGroupListingGroupFilter represents a listing group filter tree node in an asset group. |
| [`AssetGroupProductGroupView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetGroupProductGroupView) | An asset group product group view. |
| [`AssetGroupSignal`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetGroupSignal) | AssetGroupSignal represents a signal in an asset group. The existence of a signal tells the performance max campaign who's most likely to convert. Performance Max uses the signal to look for new people with similar or stronger intent to find conversions across Search, Display, Video, and more. |
| [`AssetGroupTopCombinationView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetGroupTopCombinationView) | A view on the usage of asset group asset top combinations. |
| [`AssetPolicySummary`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetPolicySummary) | Contains policy information for an asset. |
| [`AssetSet`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetSet) | An asset set representing a collection of assets. Use AssetSetAsset to link an asset to the asset set. |
| [`AssetSet.HotelPropertyData`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetSet.HotelPropertyData) | For Performance Max for travel goals campaigns with a Hotel Center account link. Read-only. |
| [`AssetSet.MerchantCenterFeed`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetSet.MerchantCenterFeed) | Merchant ID and Feed Label from Google Merchant Center. |
| [`AssetSet.TravelFeedData`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetSet.TravelFeedData) | Travel Feed data. Read-only |
| [`AssetSetAsset`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetSetAsset) | AssetSetAsset is the link between an asset and an asset set. Adding an AssetSetAsset links an asset with an asset set. |
| [`AssetSetTypeView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/AssetSetTypeView) | An asset set type view. This view reports non-overcounted metrics for each asset set type. Child asset set types are not included in this report. Their stats are aggregated under the parent asset set type. |
| [`Audience`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Audience) | Audience is an effective targeting option that lets you intersect different segment attributes, such as detailed demographics and affinities, to create audiences that represent sections of your target segments. |
| [`BackgroundCheckVerificationArtifact`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BackgroundCheckVerificationArtifact) | A proto holding information specific to local services background check. |
| [`BatchJob`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BatchJob) | A list of mutates being processed asynchronously. The mutates are uploaded by the user. The mutates themselves aren't readable and the results of the job can only be read using BatchJobService.ListBatchJobResults. |
| [`BatchJob.BatchJobMetadata`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BatchJob.BatchJobMetadata) | Additional information about the batch job. This message is also used as metadata returned in batch job Long Running Operations. |
| [`BiddingDataExclusion`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BiddingDataExclusion) | Represents a bidding data exclusion. Bidding data exclusions can be set in client accounts only, and cannot be used in manager accounts. See "About data exclusions" at <https://support.google.com/google-ads/answer/10370710>. Note: A customer account can have a maximum of 500 active bidding data exclusions. |
| [`BiddingSeasonalityAdjustment`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BiddingSeasonalityAdjustment) | Represents a bidding seasonality adjustment. Cannot be used in manager accounts. See "About seasonality adjustments" at <https://support.google.com/google-ads/answer/10369906>. |
| [`BiddingStrategy`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BiddingStrategy) | A bidding strategy. |
| [`BiddingStrategySimulation`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BiddingStrategySimulation) | A bidding strategy simulation. Supported combinations of simulation type and simulation modification method are detailed below respectively. 1. TARGET_CPA - UNIFORM 2. TARGET_ROAS - UNIFORM |
| [`BillingSetup`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BillingSetup) | A billing setup, which associates a payments account and an advertiser. A billing setup is specific to one advertiser. |
| [`BillingSetup.PaymentsAccountInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BillingSetup.PaymentsAccountInfo) | Container of payments account information for this billing. |
| [`BusinessRegistrationCheckVerificationArtifact`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BusinessRegistrationCheckVerificationArtifact) | A proto holding information specific to a local services business registration check. |
| [`BusinessRegistrationDocument`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BusinessRegistrationDocument) | A proto holding information specific to a local services business registration document. |
| [`BusinessRegistrationNumber`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/BusinessRegistrationNumber) | A proto holding information specific to a local services business registration number. |
| [`CallReportingSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/CallReportingSetting) | Call reporting setting for a customer. Only mutable in an `update` operation. |
| [`CallView`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/CallView) | A call view that includes data for call tracking of call-only ads or call extensions. |
| [`Campaign`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign) | A campaign. |
| [`Campaign.AiMaxSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.AiMaxSetting) | Settings for AI Max in Search and Shopping campaigns. |
| [`Campaign.AiMaxSetting.AiMaxBundlingRequired`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.AiMaxSetting.AiMaxBundlingRequired) | Enum describing whether AI Max must be enabled to serve and update text asset automation and brand list features newly bundled with AI Max. |

| [`Campaign.AssetAutomationSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.AssetAutomationSetting) | Asset automation setting contains pair of AssetAutomationType and the asset automation opt-in/out status |
| [`Campaign.AudienceSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.AudienceSetting) | Settings for the audience targeting. |
| [`Campaign.BrandGuidelines`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.BrandGuidelines) | Settings that control the visual appearance of your brand in a campaign's automatically generated assets and formats. Only applicable to Performance Max campaigns. |
| [`Campaign.CategoryBid`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.CategoryBid) | Category bids in LocalServicesReportingCampaignSettings. |

| [`Campaign.DynamicSearchAdsSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.DynamicSearchAdsSetting) | The setting for controlling Dynamic Search Ads (DSA). |
| [`Campaign.GeoTargetTypeSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.GeoTargetTypeSetting) | Represents a collection of settings related to ads geotargeting. |
| [`Campaign.HotelSettingInfo`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.HotelSettingInfo) | Campaign-level settings for hotel ads. |
| [`Campaign.LocalCampaignSetting`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.LocalCampaignSetting) | Campaign setting for local campaigns. |
| [`Campaign.LocalServicesCampaignSettings`](https://developers.google.com/google-ads/api/reference/rpc/v25/Campaign.LocalServicesCampaignSettings) | Settings for LocalServicesCampaign subresource. |
